# **## Payment Disappear - Service Got Disconnected** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that Payment disappear - service got disconnected.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Payment may still be processing or awaiting posting 

- Payment could have been applied to a different account or billing period 

- A bank or payment processor may have delayed the transaction 

- Billing records may not have synchronized correctly 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the customer identity and billing account 

2. Confirm the payment date, amount, and payment method 

3. Request the receipt, confirmation number, or transaction reference 

4. Check the payment status in the billing and payment systems 

5. Correct any posting or allocation error and recalculate the balance 

6. Restore affected service when payment is confirmed 

7. Follow up after the account balance and service status are updated 

# **## Isp Charging For Arbitrary Data Limits With Overage Fees** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that ISP Charging for arbitrary data limits with overage fees.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A recurring charge or fee may have been generated automatically 

- A promotional rate may have expired or changed 

- An adjustment may have been applied to the wrong billing period 

- The displayed amount may differ because of taxes or account changes 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the account holder and disputed billing period 

2. Review the itemized bill and recent account changes 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, promotion, or agreement 

4. Check whether taxes, fees, credits, or adjustments were included 

5. Reverse or correct an unsupported charge when appropriate 

6. Issue an eligible credit or refund and update the account 

7. Confirm the corrected balance with the customer

# **## Billing After Service Was Asked To Be Disconnected** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that Billing after service was asked to be disconnected.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- The account balance may contain an outdated billing entry 

- A plan or account change may not have synchronized 

- A credit, adjustment, or refund may still be pending 

- The billing statement may have been generated before the latest update 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Authenticate the customer and open the affected billing record 

2. Identify the exact billing period and disputed amount 

3. Review account changes, credits, adjustments, and previous statements 

4. Compare the billing ledger with the current service plan 

5. Correct inaccurate entries or apply the appropriate adjustment 

6. Reissue or update the statement if required 

7. Confirm the final balance and next billing date

- A promotional rate may have expired or changed 

- An adjustment may have been applied to the wrong billing period 

- The displayed amount may differ because of taxes or account changes 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the account holder and disputed billing period 

2. Review the itemized bill and recent account changes 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, promotion, or agreement 

4. Check whether taxes, fees, credits, or adjustments were included 

5. Reverse or correct an unsupported charge when appropriate 

6. Issue an eligible credit or refund and update the account 

7. Confirm the corrected balance with the customer 

# **## Billing Dispute & Poor Customer Service** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that Billing Dispute & Poor Customer Service.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- The account balance may contain an outdated billing entry 

- A plan or account change may not have synchronized 

- A credit, adjustment, or refund may still be pending 

- The billing statement may have been generated before the latest update 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Authenticate the customer and open the affected billing record 

2. Identify the exact billing period and disputed amount 

3. Review account changes, credits, adjustments, and previous statements 

4. Compare the billing ledger with the current service plan 

5. Correct inaccurate entries or apply the appropriate adjustment 

6. Reissue or update the statement if required 

7. Confirm the final balance and next billing date

# **## Comcast pricing scam** 

Complaint: Customer reports being charged or quoted an unfair or misleading Comcast price. Common Causes: 

- Unexpected price increase 

- Promotional price expired 

- Incorrect pricing information provided 

- Additional fees not clearly disclosed 

- Billing system pricing error 

Responsible Department: Billing (primary) · Customer Service · Finance 

Resolution Steps: 

1. Verify customer identity and account information 

2. Review the customer's current bill and pricing plan 

3. Compare the charged price with the agreed or advertised price 

4. Check whether a promotion or discount expired 

5. Correct unauthorized or incorrect charges 

6. Apply eligible credits or adjustments 

7. Escalate suspected deceptive pricing to the appropriate department 

8. Follow up with the customer after the billing correction 

# **## Fraudulent charges by Comcast** 

Complaint: Customer reports unauthorized or fraudulent charges appearing on their Comcast account. 

Common Causes: 

- Unauthorized services or purchases 

- Billing system error 

- Charges applied to the wrong account 

- Account compromised 

- Incorrect fees or adjustments 

Responsible Department: Billing (primary) · Finance · Customer Service · Fraud 

Resolution Steps: 

1. Verify customer identity and account information 

2. Review the disputed charges and billing history 

3. Confirm when and how the charges were created 

4. Determine whether the charges were authorized 

5. Remove or correct unauthorized charges when appropriate 

6. Check the account for additional suspicious activity 

7. Escalate suspected fraud to the Fraud department 

8. Confirm the correction with the customer 

# **## Consumer Fraud — Sell at One Price, Then Advise Price is Higher** 

Complaint: Customer reports being offered a product or service at one price and later being told that the actual price is higher. 

Common Causes: 

- Promotional pricing not properly applied 

- Incorrect sales information 

- Hidden or additional fees 

- Pricing changed between sales and billing 

- Miscommunication between sales and billing 

Responsible Department: Billing (primary) · Customer Service · Sales · Finance 

Resolution Steps: 

1. Verify customer and account information 

2. Review the original quoted or advertised price 

3. Check sales records, promotions, and agreements 

4. Compare the quoted price with the final billed amount 

5. Identify any additional fees or conditions 

6. Correct the bill if the customer was incorrectly charged 

7. Escalate repeated or suspected deceptive pricing practices 

8. Confirm the final price with the customer 

# **## Fraudulent billing by Comcast** 

Complaint: Customer reports that Comcast issued incorrect, unauthorized, or fraudulent billing charges. Common Causes: 

- Incorrect billing calculation 

- Unauthorized charges 

- Services incorrectly added 

- Billing system error 

- Charges assigned to the wrong account 

Responsible Department: Billing (primary) · Finance · Customer Service · Fraud 

Resolution Steps: 

1. Verify identity and account information 

2. Review the disputed bill and account history 

3. Identify the source of each disputed charge 

4. Verify whether the services and charges were authorized 

5. Correct or remove invalid charges 

6. Apply refunds or credits when appropriate 

7. Escalate suspected fraudulent activity 

8. Confirm the corrected bill with the customer 

# **## Comcast fraudualent billing** 

Complaint: Customer reports fraudulent or incorrect charges on their Comcast bill. 

Common Causes: 

- Unauthorized charges 

- Incorrect service charges 

- Billing system error 

- Duplicate charges 

- Charges assigned to the wrong account 

Responsible Department: Billing (primary) · Finance · Customer Service · Fraud 

Resolution Steps: 

1. Verify customer identity and account information 

2. Review the disputed billing statement 

3. Identify and verify the disputed charges 

4. Check whether services were authorized 

5. Remove or correct invalid charges 

6. Process eligible credits or refunds 

7. Escalate suspected fraud for investigation 

8. Follow up after the billing issue is resolved 

# **## Comcast keeps changing bill and every time gives new reasons!!** 

**Complaint:** comcast keeps changing bill and every time gives new reasons 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Incorrect or unexpected charge on the account 

- Pricing or plan information not applied correctly 

- Fee or adjustment not clearly explained 

- Billing records not updated after an account change 

**Responsible Department:** Billing · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Review the customer's account and billing history. 

2. Identify and verify the specific disputed charge or price. 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, agreement, or account change. 

4. Correct, remove, or refund an incorrect charge when appropriate. 

5. Explain the final billing result and any applicable terms to the customer.

# **Common Causes:** 

- Incorrect or unexpected charge on the account 

- Pricing or plan information not applied correctly 

- Fee or adjustment not clearly explained 

- Billing records not updated after an account change 

**Responsible Department:** Billing · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Review the customer's account and billing history. 

2. Identify and verify the specific disputed charge or price. 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, agreement, or account change. 

4. Correct, remove, or refund an incorrect charge when appropriate. 

5. Explain the final billing result and any applicable terms to the customer.

# **Common Causes:** 

- Incorrect or unexpected charge on the account 

- Pricing or plan information not applied correctly 

- Fee or adjustment not clearly explained 

- Billing records not updated after an account change 

# **Responsible Department:** Billing · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Review the customer's account and billing history. 

2. Identify and verify the specific disputed charge or price. 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, agreement, or account change. 

4. Correct, remove, or refund an incorrect charge when appropriate. 

5. Explain the final billing result and any applicable terms to the customer.

- Pricing or plan information not applied correctly 

- Fee or adjustment not clearly explained 

- Billing records not updated after an account change 

**Responsible Department:** Billing · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Review the customer's account and billing history. 

2. Identify and verify the specific disputed charge or price. 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, agreement, or account change. 

4. Correct, remove, or refund an incorrect charge when appropriate. 

5. Explain the final billing result and any applicable terms to the customer.

# **## Internet Connection Delibrately Slowed Down** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that Internet connection delibrately slowed down.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A recurring charge or fee may have been generated automatically 

- A promotional rate may have expired or changed 

- An adjustment may have been applied to the wrong billing period 

- The displayed amount may differ because of taxes or account changes 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the account holder and disputed billing period 

2. Review the itemized bill and recent account changes 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, promotion, or agreement 

4. Check whether taxes, fees, credits, or adjustments were included 

5. Reverse or correct an unsupported charge when appropriate 

6. Issue an eligible credit or refund and update the account 

7. Confirm the corrected balance with the customer 

# **## Service Not Disconnected As Requested Overcharged And Overpaid As A** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that service not disconnected as requested. overcharged and overpaid as a result.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A recurring charge or fee may have been generated automatically 

- A promotional rate may have expired or changed 

- An adjustment may have been applied to the wrong billing period 

- The displayed amount may differ because of taxes or account changes 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the account holder and disputed billing period 

2. Review the itemized bill and recent account changes 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, promotion, or agreement 

4. Check whether taxes, fees, credits, or adjustments were included 

5. Reverse or correct an unsupported charge when appropriate 

6. Issue an eligible credit or refund and update the account 

7. Confirm the corrected balance with the customer 

# **## Billing Issues** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that billing issues.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- The account balance may contain an outdated billing entry 

- A plan or account change may not have synchronized 

- A credit, adjustment, or refund may still be pending 

- The billing statement may have been generated before the latest update 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Authenticate the customer and open the affected billing record 

2. Identify the exact billing period and disputed amount 

3. Review account changes, credits, adjustments, and previous statements 

4. Compare the billing ledger with the current service plan 

5. Correct inaccurate entries or apply the appropriate adjustment 

6. Reissue or update the statement if required 

7. Confirm the final balance and next billing date

7. Confirm the final balance and next billing date

- A credit, adjustment, or refund may still be pending 

- The billing statement may have been generated before the latest update 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Authenticate the customer and open the affected billing record 

2. Identify the exact billing period and disputed amount 

3. Review account changes, credits, adjustments, and previous statements 

4. Compare the billing ledger with the current service plan 

5. Correct inaccurate entries or apply the appropriate adjustment 

6. Reissue or update the statement if required 

7. Confirm the final balance and next billing date

# **## Illegal Charges From Comcast** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that Illegal Charges from Comcast.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A recurring charge or fee may have been generated automatically 

- A promotional rate may have expired or changed 

- An adjustment may have been applied to the wrong billing period 

- The displayed amount may differ because of taxes or account changes 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:**

2. Review the itemized bill and recent account changes 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, promotion, or agreement

5. Reverse or correct an unsupported charge when appropriate 

6. Issue an eligible credit or refund and update the account 

7. Confirm the corrected balance with the customer

# **## Bill Payment** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that bill payment.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Payment may still be processing or awaiting posting 

- Payment could have been applied to a different account or billing period 

- A bank or payment processor may have delayed the transaction 

- Billing records may not have synchronized correctly 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the customer identity and billing account 

2. Confirm the payment date, amount, and payment method 

3. Request the receipt, confirmation number, or transaction reference 

4. Check the payment status in the billing and payment systems 

5. Correct any posting or allocation error and recalculate the balance 

6. Restore affected service when payment is confirmed 

7. Follow up after the account balance and service status are updated

# **## Issues With Service And Payments** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that Issues with service and payments.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Payment may still be processing or awaiting posting 

- Payment could have been applied to a different account or billing period 

- A bank or payment processor may have delayed the transaction 

- Billing records may not have synchronized correctly 

# **Responsible Department: Billing** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the customer identity and billing account 

2. Confirm the payment date, amount, and payment method 

3. Request the receipt, confirmation number, or transaction reference 

4. Check the payment status in the billing and payment systems 

5. Correct any posting or allocation error and recalculate the balance 

6. Restore affected service when payment is confirmed 

7. Follow up after the account balance and service status are updated

# **## Comcast price increase not in line with advertisement** 

**Complaint:** comcast price increase not in line with advertisement 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Incorrect or unexpected charge on the account 

- Pricing or plan information not applied correctly 

- Fee or adjustment not clearly explained 

- Billing records not updated after an account change 

**Responsible Department:** Billing · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Review the customer's account and billing history. 

2. Identify and verify the specific disputed charge or price. 

3. Compare the charge with the applicable plan, agreement, or account change. 

4. Correct, remove, or refund an incorrect charge when appropriate. 

5. Explain the final billing result and any applicable terms to the customer.

4. Correct, remove, or refund an incorrect charge when appropriate. 

5. Explain the final billing result and any applicable terms to the customer.

# **## Customer called about a billing dispute** 

# **Complaint: Customer called about a billing dispute** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Account or service issue requires additional review 

- Customer information or request was not handled correctly 

- Multiple departments may need to review the case 

- Insufficient account or case information may have delayed resolution 

**Responsible Department:** Customer Service (primary) · Billing · Technical Support 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Review the complaint and identify the affected account or service. 

2. Collect the relevant account history and supporting information. 

3. Route the issue to the appropriate responsible department. 

4. Confirm the outcome with the customer and document the resolution.
